```
<instruction> Input A: calculus
Input A is a technology that traveled historical trade routes (papermaking, gunpowder, astronomy, silk production, algebra, etc.).
Analyze: origin civilization, the journey route, cultural adaptations at each stop, timeline of spread.
Goal: Miniature Silk Road/trade route caravan scene showing technology being transported and transformed.
Rules:
- Base: 3D topographic map showing actual historical route (mountains, deserts, seas)
- Caravan: 8-12 miniature merchant/scholar figurines progressing along route with pack animals
- Each stop: micro-diorama of how that civilization adapted the technology
* Origin point: technology in pure form with inventor culture's aesthetic
* Midpoint stops: hybrid versions showing cultural fusion
* Destination: fully transformed/adapted version
- Include: the technology itself transforming—paper becoming different textures, numbers changing symbol systems, etc.
- Obstacles: miniature barriers that were overcome (mountain passes, hostile territories, language barriers shown as scroll fragments in different scripts)
- Time markers: seasons/years labeled along route
- Materials: actual sand/terrain materials, tiny textiles from different cultures, brass caravans
- Lighting: journey from dawn (origin) to dusk (destination) across the scene
Output: ONE image, 21:9 ultra-wide to capture full journey, epic historical adventure photography
</instruction>
```

### 1290 - 数学符号的巨石构造

**作者**: Gadgetify ([链接](https://x.com/Gdgtify))
**风格**: cinematic
**标签**: Megalithic Geological Formation, Ancient Mystery, Titanic Scale, National Geographic Expedition, Photorealistic Matte Painting

```
<instructions> Analyze Input Variable: Input A is an Abstract Symbol, Mathematical Constant, or Letter (e.g., Pi, Infinity, The letter 'A'). Translate the 2D glyph into a Megalithic Geological Formation:
Material: Weathered Sandstone, Granite, or Basalt.
Texture: The surface must show centuries of erosion, cracks, moss growth in crevices, and wind damage. It cannot look like a perfect 3D primitive; it must look carved by nature or ancients.
Detail: The surface is etched with faint, ancient carvings (runes, hieroglyphs, or the endless digits of the number itself) that look like forgotten languages.
The "Immutable" Container (The Environment): Goal: A "National Geographic" Expedition Shot.
Location: A vast, arid Desert Canyon or Valley of the Kings.
Topography: Towering cliffs in the background, sand dunes in the foreground.
Atmosphere: Heavy atmospheric haze, swirling sand dust, and a sense of ancient mystery.
The Central Composition (The Monument):
Placement: The Symbol (Input A) stands colossally in the center, acting as a natural bridge or archway.
Scale: It must be Titanic. It should dwarf the surrounding canyon walls.
Structure: If the symbol has a hole (like Pi or A), light must pass through it dramatically.
The Narrative Scale (The Caravan):
Crucial Constraint: To sell the scale, include a Micro-Scale Caravan at the very base of the monument.
Figures: Tiny, robed nomads, camels, or pack animals walking in a line.
Size Relationship: The figures should be no taller than a single "toenail" of the giant structure. They emphasize that this object is hundreds of feet tall.
Lighting & Atmosphere:
Lighting:  Crepuscular Rays. Strong beams of sunlight breaking through storm clouds or gaps in the rock, hitting the monument.
Palette: Earth tones. Ochre, Tan, Burnt Orange, contrasted with the dark shadows of the crevices.
Vibe: discovery of a lost civilization
Output: ONE image, 1:1 or 4:5 Aspect Ratio, Photorealistic Matte Painting, Epic Scale, Cinematic Lighting. </instruction>
```

```
Invention = [User Choice, e.g., Electricity, The Airplane, The Telephone]
Phase 1: Historical Conflict Analysis
1.  Identify Rivals: Determine the two main historical figures associated with this invention (e.g., Tesla vs. Edison, Wright Bros vs. Curtiss).
2.  Identify The Tech: Distinguish their competing methods (e.g., AC Coils vs. DC Bulbs).
3.  Identify The Era: Select appropriate "Giant" background clutter (e.g., Victorian newspapers, 1920s fountain pens).
Phase 2: Visual Execution
Goal: A "Macro Diorama" of a Rivalry. A messy, highly detailed flat-lay of a shared historical workspace where the two rivals exist as tiny living figurines battling over their creation.
Rules:
The Stage: A large, yellowed Patent Diagram or Blueprint lies flat on a wooden desk. The paper is wrinkled and covered in scribbled corrections.
The Tiny Rivals: Two hyper-realistic miniature figurines (Inventor A and Rival B) are standing on opposite sides of the document. They are positioned at their own tiny wooden desks or workstations on top of the paper.
The Conflict: They are gesturing aggressively at each other. The Invention itself is physically emerging from the center of the paper between them (e.g., a glowing 3D lightbulb or a spinning biplane propeller), acting as the barrier or prize they are fighting over.
The "Giant" Terrain (Surroundings): The scene is surrounded by massive, period-accurate objects acting as a landscape:
A "Giant" wall of newspapers with headlines about their legal battles.
Massive burnt fuses or broken prototypes scattered like boulders.
A spilled cup of coffee creating a "lake" near one of the figures.
Giant tools (calipers, screwdrivers) casting long shadows.
Perspective: Slightly angled 45-degree macro shot looking down at the patent.
Texture: Dust motes, scratched mahogany wood, paper grain, metallic reflections.
Lighting: Cinematic Macro Lighting. A warm desk lamp illuminates the scene, but the Invention in the center emits its own light (e.g., blue electrical arcs vs. yellow filament glow), lighting the faces of the tiny figures.
Output: ONE image, 4:5 aspect ratio, photorealistic historical macro photography, tilt-shift effect.
</instructions>
```

```
その際、添付された参照画像のキャラクターを、**「ジオラマの世界観に合わせた観光ガイド役のフィギュア」**として配置してください。キャラクターの一貫性を維持しつつ、ジオラマの一部として違和感なく溶け込ませてください（例：建物の横で案内している、ベンチに座っている等）。

3.  **[ステップ3: 空間演出]**: ジオラマは正方形の木製台座の上に構築します。全体を照らす暖色のスタジオライティングを想定し、被写界深度（ボケ味）を調整して、視線を中心的なランドマークに誘導してください。
```

```
▼ 生成プロセスへの指示 (Thinking Process)
Nano Banana Proの推論能力を活かし、描画前に以下の思考プロセスを実行してください。

世界設定： 大陸の中央を東西に走る巨大な山脈「竜の背骨（Dragon's Spine）」によって、世界は北と南に分断されている。
北部「氷の帝国グラキエス」:
気候：極寒の永久凍土。
地形：険しい氷河と針葉樹林。
首都：山脈の麓にある要塞都市「冬の牙」。
```

### 1631 - 纳米香蕉Pro系统提示：寻找极限，实现多宝石协同工作以完成复杂工作流程

**作者**: Emily ([链接](https://x.com/IamEmily2050))
**风格**: cinematic
**标签**: high-fidelity, cinematic-quality, hyper-realistic, atmospheric lighting, set design

```
for Nano banana Pro, I can't find the limit yet, we just need to have multiple Gems working together so we can do a complex workflow.
*Task: Generate a high-fidelity, cinematic-quality environment render based on a narrative brief.**
**Objective:**
Act as a professional set designer and director of photography. Given a short narrative description, generate a single, hyper-realistic image that perfectly captures the scene's mood, atmosphere, and visual detail.
**Input Parameters:**
1.  **Scene Description:** (e.g., "A lone detective sits in a dimly lit, rain-streaked diner booth at 3 AM, the neon sign outside casting a harsh red glow on his face.")
2.  **Visual Style/Era:** (e.g., "Neo-Noir," "Cyberpunk," "1950s Technicolor," "Gothic Horror")
3.  **Camera/Lens:** (e.g., "Wide-angle shot," "Close-up, 85mm lens," "Low-angle, dramatic perspective")
**Core Deliverables:**
1.  **Photorealistic Render:** A single, high-resolution image that is indistinguishable from a photograph.
2.  **Atmospheric Lighting:** The lighting must be the primary driver of the mood, utilizing complex techniques (e.g., volumetric fog, rim lighting, high-contrast chiaroscuro).
3.  **Compositional Storytelling:** The composition must guide the viewer's eye and convey the narrative tension or emotion of the scene description.
4.  **Detail & Texture:** Extreme attention to surface detail (e.g., wet asphalt, worn leather, reflections in glass).
**Workflow:**
1.  **Analyze:** Deconstruct the narrative brief into its core emotional, environmental, and lighting components.
2.  **Design:** Select the optimal camera angle and lighting setup to maximize the cinematic impact.
3.  **Render:** Generate the final image, ensuring the specified Visual Style/Era is authentically represented.
```

### 1703 - 大唐长安插画

**作者**: imaxichuhai ([链接](https://x.com/imaxichuhai))
**风格**: artistic
**标签**: 插画地图, 唐代仕女画, 工笔重彩, 散点透视, 大唐长安

```
画幅比例16:9，一幅令人叹为观止的插画地图学杰作，描绘了唐代首都长安的地图。整个画面以唐代仕女画的精致风格呈现，令人联想到绘画大师周昉的作品。媒材是在陈旧、细腻的绢本上使用工笔重彩。\n\n**构图与透视:**\n地图采用“散点透视”的长卷形式。城市感觉更像一幅生动的织锦，而非僵硬的蓝图。朱雀大街作为画面的中轴线。\n\n**插画与字体细节:**\n- **作为微型场景的地标:** 关键地点是小巧、精致的叙事场景：\n- **大明宫:** 宫廷仕女在园林中演奏乐器。\n- **西市:** 粟特商人与贵族妇女交易丝绸。\n- **曲江池:** 仕女们正在进行“曲水流觞”。\n- **作为地图元素的优雅人物:** 优雅的唐代仕女被用作装饰元素，她们的姿态和袖带引导着观众的视线。\n\n**一百零八坊 - 高级指令:**\n一百零八坊由一个个优雅的手绘长方形印章来代表。核心目标是用貌似可信的、具有历史感的文字填充这些印章。\n- **指令:** AI不必尝试渲染全部108个独一无二的坊名，而是必须从提供的“样本集”中学习，并用相似的、合理的、不重复的双字坊名去填充每一个印章。\n- **著名坊名样本集 (用于风格参考):**\n“平康坊”、“崇仁坊”、“兴庆坊”、“道政坊”、“长兴坊”、“永崇坊”、“亲仁坊”、“永宁坊”、“怀远坊”、“延康坊”、“金城坊”、“布政坊”。\n- **风格强制要求:** 所有印章内的文字必须是优美、纤细、典雅的“小篆”字体。结构应为“[某][某]坊”。\n\n**主标题与标签:**\n- **主标题:** “大唐长安”以雄浑大气的隶书，竖排书写在右上角。\n- **标签:** 街道（“朱雀大街”）和河流（“渭水”）的名称，以娟秀的行书直接写在绢本背景上，并顺着路径的曲线流动。\n\n**美学与氛围:**\n调色板丰富而华丽（朱砂、石绿、石青、金箔）。线条如“游丝描”般细劲流畅。整体氛围是一种繁华、诗意、优雅的感觉。\n\n**负面提示词:**\n英文, 罗马字母, 3D, 现代地图, 网格布局, 几何形状, 电脑字体, 西方艺术风格, 卡通, 简单, 极简, 空白区域, 水印, 糟糕的书法, 乱码。
```

### 1705 - 成都旅游地图

**作者**: imaxichuhai ([链接](https://x.com/imaxichuhai))
**风格**: illustration
**标签**: 成都手绘旅游地图, 吉卜力风格, 水彩钢笔淡彩, 等轴测视角, 手写书法

```
画幅比例16:9，一张迷人而精致的成都手绘旅游地图，具有吉卜力工作室艺术设定集般的风格。整个画面是在一张有纹理的、陈旧的羊皮纸上的水彩和钢笔淡彩插画。整体美学风格异想天开、充满活力和生活气息。\n\n地图布局与风格:\n地图以风格化的等轴测视角，展示了成都的核心地标，这些地标被描绘成可爱、精细的微缩建筑和图标（例如，熊猫基地的熊猫、锦里的灯笼、人民公园的茶馆）。布局是有机的、自由流动的，而非基于僵硬的网格。\n\n至关重要的字体排版挑战 (高难度部分):\n所有文字必须以一种优美的、略带不完美感的手写书法风格（行楷）呈现，看起来就像是用绘制插图的同一支钢笔写出来的。\n1.  **主标题:** 主标题“成都慢行图”被艺术性地写在顶部一条飘逸的缎带上。\n2.  **带角度的趣味标签:** 每个地标图标旁边，都以俏皮的、略带倾斜的方式写着它的名字（例如：“宽窄巷子”、“武侯祠”、“杜甫草堂”）。文字需要感觉是有机放置的，而不是死板的水平线。\n3.  **沿路径弯曲的文字:** 一条风格化的锦江贯穿地图。河流的名字“锦江”二字，需要沿着河流的弯曲路径优雅地书写。这是一个关键测试点。\n4.  **融合性的注释:** 地图上散布着小小的、异想天开的注释，这些注释结合了文字和图标。例如：\n    - 一条写着“吃火锅!”的注释，旁边画着一个小小的红辣椒。\n    - 一个“喝茶咯!”的标签，旁边有一个冒着热气的微型茶杯图标。\n    - 区域名称“锦江区”被写在一个手绘的云朵形状里。\n5.  **手绘图例:** 在一个角落，有一个手绘的“图例”方框，里面有小图标（如茶杯、熊猫脸、寺庙屋顶）和它们对应的手写标签。\n6.  **印章:** 一枚红色的、仿佛手工篆刻风格的印章，印着“蓉”字（成都的简称），被盖在角落，并与边框有轻微的重叠。\n\n美学:\n一幅插画地图学的杰作。文字和插图的融合必须天衣无缝。水彩效果要柔和，有可见的水渍和纹理，而钢笔线条则要自信而生动。整体感觉温暖、诱人，充满个性。\n\n负面提示词:\n电脑字体, 计算机生成的文字, 直线, 僵硬的网格, 完美对齐, 只有水平文字, 文字浮层, 照片, 3D, 极简, 通用图标, 拼写错误, 乱码, 水印。
```

### 1716 - 动态高速追逐：山间公路上两辆黑色运动跑车较量

**作者**: Iqra Saifi ([链接](https://x.com/IqraSaifiii))
**风格**: cinematic
**标签**: high-speed chase, European sports coupes, mountain highway, golden hour, motion blur

```
Veo-3.1 Fast in @GeminiApp
A dynamic, high-speed chase featuring two modern, black European sports coupes (like a BMW M4 or similar), racing down a scenic, winding mountain highway. The clip focuses on the moment the rear car aggressively overtakes the lead car. The action should feel fast, powerful, and exhilarating.
Camera Movement (Crucial):
* Start (0-2 seconds): Begin with a low-angle, aggressive tracking shot held tightly to the rear bumper and glowing LED taillights of the trailing car (the one closest to the camera in the image). Show the road rushing underneath with intense motion blur.
* Mid-Sequence (2-6 seconds): The camera executes a smooth, rapid cinematic pull-out and arcing pan to the left, following the trailing car as it surges forward into the adjacent lane to overtake. Briefly capture both cars side-by-side, emphasizing the motion blur on the background trees and road texture.
* End (6-8 seconds): The camera stabilizes and locks onto a mid-to-high angle just ahead of the new lead car, showcasing the open road ahead and the sunlight streaking through the trees. The final two seconds hold this dynamic, fast-moving perspective.
Style & Aesthetics:
* Aesthetic: Cinematic, hyper-realistic, high-fidelity game engine render quality (like Unreal Engine 5 or Forza Motorsport).
* Color Palette: Rich, warm autumn colors (deep golds, oranges, browns) contrasted with the deep black cars and vibrant blue sky.
* Lighting: Golden Hour lighting. Intense, directional sun flare and volumetric light shafts cutting through the pine and deciduous trees on the right side of the frame, creating dramatic shadows and highlights across the road and the cars' bodies.
* Motion: Extreme depth-of-field and radial motion blur applied to the background and the road to amplify the sense of speed, keeping the cars relatively sharp.
```

### 1968 - 天空之城：融合多国景观的奇幻漂浮岛

**作者**: Keng哥 ([链接](https://x.com/langzihan))
**风格**: cinematic
**标签**: fantasy floating island, National Spirit Architect, Nature-Architecture Symbiosis, Avatar-style base, majestic composition

```
👇
#AIart #NanoBananaPro
提示词：
# Role Definition
You are a **National Spirit Architect (国家精神造景师)**. Your goal is to create a majestic, **fantasy floating island** that amalgamates the most iconic natural landscapes (famous mountains, rivers, lakes) and architectural landmarks of a specific **Country/Region**.
# Core Competency
**CRITICAL VISUAL STRATEGY (The Harmonious Fusion):**
1.  **Abandon Map Logic:** Do NOT follow the real-world map. You are creating an artistic "Best Of" composition. Place the snowy mountains behind the tropical lakes; place the capital\'s palace next to the ancient natural wonders.
2.  **Nature-Architecture Symbiosis:** Architecture should not just sit on top; it should be nestled **into** the mountains, perched **on** the cliffs, or surrounded by the winding rivers. The transition between man-made structures and nature must be organic.
3.  **The "Avatar" Base:** Retain the **tapering, inverted mountain base**. The island looks like a chunk of ancient land lifted by magic, with hanging vines, waterfalls, and rugged rock strata at the bottom.
4.  **Flowing Water:** A stylized river or lake system must weave through the composition to connect the disparate elements visually.
# Work Process (Internal "Chain of Thought")
Based on the provided **{TARGET_COUNTRY}**:
1.  **Select Icons:** Identify the top 3-4 visual symbols (e.g., for China: Great Wall, Forbidden City, Yellow Mountains, Yangtze River; for France: Alps, Eiffel Tower, Vineyards, Seine).
2.  **Composition Strategy:**
* *Back:* High peaks/Mountains (The Backdrop).
* *Mid:* Major Architectural Complexes (The Core).
* *Front:* Waterways/Lakes and smaller cultural details (The Vibe).
3.  **Fusion:** Ensure the Great Wall winds *through* the mountains, or the castle sits *on* the cliff.
# Output Format (The Final Prompt)
You will output a single prompt block optimized for **National Landscape Fantasy**:
---
**Prompt Structure:**
**[1. The Grand Composition]**
A **breathtaking, high-angle fantasy isometric view** of **{TARGET_COUNTRY}** visualized as a **massive, singular floating continent**. The image abandons realistic geography in favor of an **artistic amalgamation** of the country's most famous mountains, rivers, and landmarks blended into one harmonious island.
**[2. The Natural & Architectural Fusion]**
The island is a dense tapestry of **{TARGET_COUNTRY}**'s soul:
* **[Zone 1 - The Majestic Backdrop]:** The rear of the island features **{TARGET_COUNTRY}'s most famous mountain ranges and natural peaks** [e.g., snowy peaks, karst mountains, or rolling hills].
* **[Zone 2 - The Cultural Core]:** Nestled among the mountains and hills are **iconic historical and modern architectures** of {TARGET_COUNTRY}. [Describe specific landmarks like Palaces, Towers, or Temples] are integrated into the terrain—built into cliffs or sitting in valleys—not just placed flat.
* **[Zone 3 - The Water Soul]:** A winding river or serene lake [representative of {TARGET_COUNTRY}'s famous waters] flows through the center, reflecting the architecture. The water cascades off the edge of the floating island into **misty, dreamlike waterfalls**.
**[3. The Floating Base (The Organic Anchor)]**
The landmass floats in the sky with a **rugged, inverted-mountain shape**:
* **The Underside:** Ancient rock formations, **giant hanging roots**, and dripping water. It creates a silhouette of a mystical flying island. **NO flat concrete slabs.**
* **The Atmosphere:** Clouds weave through the mountains and under the island.
**[4. The Branding & Atmosphere]**
**Epic Fantasy Realism.** The lighting is **magical and dramatic** (e.g., rays of god-light hitting the peaks). **Massive 3D text "{TARGET_COUNTRY}"** is integrated into the foreground landscape (e.g., carved into a rock or floating over the water). 8k resolution, highly detailed, octane render, unreal engine 5 style. --no map borders, flat edges, scientific diagram, square box --ar 16:9 --stylize 900 --v 6.0
---
```

```
v2:
# Role Definition
You are a **World-Class Text-to-Image Prompt Engineer** and **Visual Narrative Director**. Your expertise lies in translating abstract literary or cinematic titles into highly structured, technically precise image prompts for AI generators (Midjourney v6, DALL-E 3).
# Core Competency
You do NOT just list objects. You **engineer a cohesive visual ecosystem**. You reject random object scattering. You ensure that every element in the scene is geographically and narratively connected by a strong compositional "spine" (e.g., a river, a road, a timeline, a beam of light).
# Work Process (Internal "Chain of Thought")
When the user provides a **[Title]**, you must execute the following logic before generating the prompt:
1.  **Deconstruct the Narrative:** Identify the core environment (e.g., The Shire, Cyberpunk Neo-Tokyo) and the central theme.
2.  **Design the "Spine":** Establish a unifying physical element that flows through the image (left-to-right or foreground-to-background) to guide the viewer\'s eye and connect the scenes.
3.  **Cast the Actors:** Select 5-7 key characters. Assign them **dynamic, interactive actions** specific to their fate. They must be interacting with the "Spine" or their immediate environment, not just standing there.
4.  **Apply the Aesthetic:** Enforce the "Exquisite Miniature Claymation" style with specific technical keywords (tilt-shift, octane render, tactile textures).
# Output Format (The Final Prompt)
You will output a single, ready-to-use English prompt block following this strict structure:
---
**Prompt Structure:**
**[1. The Macro Composition & Spine]**
A panoramic, wide-angle 4K isometric capture of an exquisite miniature claymation world representing **[Insert Title]**. The composition is anchored by a **[Insert The "Spine" Element, e.g., a winding golden river / a fractured railway track]** that flows seamlessly through the landscape, physically connecting the diverse settings from foreground to background. The layout creates a natural narrative journey.
**[2. The Living Environment]**
The terrain is a handcrafted model of **[Insert Core Setting]**. It is not empty; it is dense with life: [Insert generic environmental details, e.g., tiny smoking chimneys, waving wheat fields, moving gears]. The texture is tactile—visible fingerprints on clay, fuzzy felt grass, and resin water.
**[3. The Character Micro-Narratives (Focal Points)]**
Strategically placed along the "Spine" are dynamic character vignettes:
* **[Character A]:** Located [Location relative to spine], [Action reflecting fate - must be dynamic, e.g., running, fighting, building].
* **[Character B]:** Located [Location], [Action reflecting fate].
* **[Character C]:** Located [Location], [Action reflecting fate].
* **[Character D]:** Located [Location], [Action reflecting fate].
* **[Character E]:** Located [Location], [Action reflecting fate].
**[4. The Branding & Atmosphere]**
In the immediate foreground, the title "**[Insert Title]**" is elegantly carved into a **[Material fitting the theme, e.g., rough wood / rusted metal / neon sign]** baseplate. The lighting is **[Lighting Style, e.g., Cinematic Golden Hour / Moody Cyberpunk Blue]**, casting long shadows and highlighting the miniature textures. Tilt-shift photography effect. --ar 16:9 --stylize 250 --v 6.0

# User Input
The user will provide only the **[Title]**. You will perform the engineering and output the final prompt.
```

```
V3:
# Role Definition
You are the **Master Architect of Miniature Worlds**. Your goal is to create wide-angle, interconnected claymation dioramas.
# The "Iceberg" Rule (CRITICAL)
When the user provides a specific scene description (e.g., "Sun Shao\'an\'s brick kiln firing"), treat this input as **only the visual anchor (approx. 30-40% of the composition)**.
* **DO NOT** fill the entire image with the user's description.
* **YOU MUST** "Zoom Out" to reveal the surrounding world.
* **YOU MUST** autonomously hallucinate logically connected scenes (Zone B & C) that are happening *simultaneously* nearby.
# Composition Strategy: The 3-Zone Layout
You must construct the scene using this layout logic:
1.  **The Spine (The Connector):** First, establish a physical line (river, winding road, mountain ridge) that runs through the entire image, physically connecting Zone A, B, and C.
2.  **Zone A (The User's Anchor):** The specific event described by the user. Place this prominently but NOT occupying the whole frame.
3.  **Zone B (The Reaction/Consequence):** Who is watching this event? Who is affected by it? (e.g., If Zone A is a celebration, Zone B might be a jealous rival watching from a distance, or a family preparing food for it).
4.  **Zone C (The Indifferent World):** Life goes on. Show characters nearby doing completely unrelated daily tasks (farming, studying) to add realism and scale.
# Workflow & Logic
1.  **Analyze User Input:** Identify the Book Title and the Specific Scene.
2.  **Determine Timeline:** based on the scene, who else should be alive and where should they be?
3.  **Draft the "Spine":** How does the geography link these people?
4.  **Generate Prompt:** Use the structure below.
# Output Structure (Strictly follow this)
**Prompt:**
**[1. Wide-Angle Context & The Spine]**
A wide-angle, 4K isometric miniature diorama of **[Insert Book Title]**. The view is zoomed out to show a vast section of the landscape, not just a single scene. A **[Insert Spine Element, e.g., dusty main road/river]** winds through the composition, physically connecting the foreground activity to the distant village/horizon.
**[2. Zone A: The Anchor Event (User's Input)]**
Located at the **[Position, e.g., riverbank/center-left]**, we see the main event: **[Refine User's Scene Description into claymation details]**. This area is active and detailed, serving as the narrative focal point.
**[3. Zone B & C: The Expanded World (AI Generated)]**
* **[Zone B - The Connection]:** Connected by the [Spine], at **[Another Location, e.g., the village entrance/hilltop]**, we see **[Character Name NOT mentioned by user]** who is **[Action related to Zone A, e.g., watching enviously / rushing to join / ignoring the noise]**.
* **[Zone C - The Background Life]:** In the distance/periphery, **[Character Name or Generic Villagers]** are **[Action unrelated to main event, e.g., tilling the fields/walking to school]**, showing that life continues.
* **[Details]:** The spaces between zones are filled with [Environmental details: crops, trees, telephone poles] to ensure organic continuity.
**[4. Title & Aesthetic]**
The title "**[Insert Book Title]**" is embedded in the foreground on a **[Material]** plaque.
Style: Exquisite 3D claymation, tilt-shift lens (focus on Zone A, slight blur on edges), warm lighting, octane render, 8k.
--ar 16:9 --stylize 250 --v 6.0
---
# User Input Format
`[Book Title]` + `[Scene Description]`
```

### 1973 - 《权力的游戏》角色经历场景聚合图片生成

**作者**: Keng哥 ([链接](https://x.com/langzihan))
**风格**: 3d
**标签**: isometric, miniature world, full-bleed, 3D typography, high-density

```
V4:
# Role Definition
You are a **Borderless Miniature World Architect**. Your goal is to create a hyper-dense, vertically stacked isometric world that feels like an **infinite slice of reality**. You must remove all artificial borders, wooden frames, or glass boxes. The landscape surface must bleed to the very edges of the image.
# Core Competency
**CRITICAL VISUAL STRATEGY (Frameless Full-Bleed):**
1.  **Eradicate the Container & Cross-Section:** STRICTLY NO baseplates, NO frames, and **NO vertical ground cross-sections or cutaways at the bottom edge**. The terrain surface itself must extend right to the bottom of the frame.
2.  **Infinite Surface Extend:** The bottom edge of the image must show the **top surface** of the terrain (e.g., grass, pavement, sand), as if the camera is looking down at the ground extending off-screen.
3.  **Integrated 3D Title:** The **[Work Title]** must be rendered as **massive, cinematic 3D Typography** standing directly ON this extended terrain surface in the immediate foreground.
4.  **High-Density Vertical Stack:** Continue to use the "Zig-Zag Ascent" method to pack 5-7 scenes from bottom to top.
# Work Process (Internal "Chain of Thought")
When provided with **[Work Title] + [Character Name]**:
1.  **Conceive the "Infinite Ground":** Imagine standing on a hill; the ground at your feet doesn\'t have a cut-off edge, it just continues. That\'s the bottom edge view.
2.  **Layout the Zig-Zag:**
* *Bottom (Foreground):* 3D Title sitting on extended ground + Beat 1 & 2.
* *Middle (Ascending):* Beat 3, 4, 5 winding upwards.
* *Top (Background):* Beat 6 & 7 fading into the atmosphere.
3.  **Text Integration:** The 3D text should cast shadows onto the terrain surface it sits upon.
# Output Format (The Final Prompt)
You will output a single prompt block optimized for **Frameless Full-Bleed High Density**:
---
**Prompt Structure:**
**[1. The Frameless Full-Bleed Composition]**
A **frameless, edge-to-edge** high-angle isometric landscape representing **[Insert Work Title]**. The image is NOT contained in a box and shows **NO vertical cross-section** at the bottom. The terrain surface **fills the entire 16:9 frame**, extending all the way to the bottom corners. The composition follows a **vertical zigzagging path** (The "Spine") connecting **7 distinct narrative layers**.
**[2. The 7-Stage Rising World (Seamless & Dense)]**
The terrain is a continuous, rising expanse:
* **[Layer 1 - Bottom Front]:** The immediate foreground surface, extending to the bottom edge. **Massive 3D text spelling "[Insert Work Title]" stands here**, planted on the [Describe terrain surface, e.g., desert sand/cobblestone street]. Beside it is [Describe Scene 1].
* **[Layer 2 - Front Right]:** The path climbs to [Describe Scene 2].
* **[Layer 3 - Mid-Left]:** Stacked above, [Describe Scene 3].
* **[Layer 4 - Center Core]:** A dense transition zone featuring [Describe Scene 4].
* **[Layer 5 - Mid-Right Elevated]:** Rising steeply to [Describe Scene 5].
* **[Layer 6 - Upper Left]:** [Describe Scene 6].
* **[Layer 7 - Top Peak]:** The highest point featuring [Describe Scene 7].
* *Note:* The layout is organic. The bottom edge shows the **continuous surface material** of the terrain, NOT a cutaway slice.
**[3. The Character's Ascent]**
**[Insert Character Name]** appears as a recurring miniature figure throughout the climb:
1.  [Action at Layer 1 near the Title]
2.  [Action at Layer 2]
3.  [Action at Layer 3]
4.  [Action at Layer 4]
5.  [Action at Layer 5]
6.  [Action at Layer 6]
7.  [Action at Layer 7]
**[4. The Branding & Atmosphere]**
**No frames, no borders, no cross-sections.** Lighting is cinematic and volumetric. The text "**[Insert Work Title]**" is rendered in **[Material Style]** 3D letters casting shadows on the ground. Tilt-shift photography, macro details, claymation texture, octane render, 8k resolution. --no wooden base, box, frame, borders, cross-section view, cutaway --ar 16:9 --stylize 750 --v 6.0
```

### 2006 - 海边情侣超现实主义3D渲染

**作者**: 李继刚 ([链接](https://x.com/lijigang))
**风格**: 3d
**标签**: 超现实主义, 微缩模型感, 粉彩系统, 体积感云朵, 情侣看海

```
1. 风格调性
超现实主义3D渲染，梦幻柔和，阳光普照，风格化现实主义，微缩模型感，电影感，宁静怀旧。
2. 视觉逻辑
- 视角：平视，接近正交投影，低视平线。
- 布局：居中构图，静态平衡。
- 几何处理：平滑、干净的3D几何体，边缘圆润，无锐角。
3. 视觉渲染
- 线条：干净、平滑的3D边缘，无手绘或像素痕迹。
- 材质：光滑、略带磨砂的玩具般质感。表面无瑕疵、无污垢。云朵具有体积感但同样柔和。
- 光影：暖色调直射光，全局照明渲染，柔和但清晰的投影。
4. 色彩系统
- 核心主色：婴儿蓝 (#A2CFFE)，柔和粉 (#F4C2C2)，奶油白 (#FDFDFA)。
- 背景色与高亮：背景为奶油色沙滩、深蓝海面与淡蓝天空的渐变。植物的自然绿与木材的暖色调作为点缀。整体为低饱和度、高明度的暖色调粉彩系统。
5. 负向约束
- 严禁超写实纹理、污渍、磨损。
- 严禁暗黑、高对比度或赛博朋克霓虹光照。
- 严禁像素艺术、低多边形或手绘草图风格。
- 严禁高饱和度或冲突的色彩。
6. 画面内容
一对情侣在海边手牵手，看海
```

```
水墨画色调,徽墨重彩,传统民俗浓厚,宁静祥和。
━━━ 冬春过渡:立春时节 (20-32%)
- 20%: 除夕夜,祠堂祭祖,灯笼高挂
- 24%: 正月舞龙灯,村民喜庆
- 28%: 雨水节气,冰融水涨,油菜花苞显现
- 32%: 春耕开始,农具准备
━━━ 春季:清明谷雨 (32-45%)
三月油菜花开如金色海洋,映衬白墙黑瓦,
南湖水清,柳绿桃红,画桥上游人如织,
村民采茶制茶,香气飘满村落,
写生的美院学生支起画架,摄影师长枪短炮,
祠堂举办春祭,古礼庄重,
嫩绿金黄色调,诗情画意,春和景明,江南春天典型。
━━━ 春夏过渡:芒种夏至 (45-57%)
- 45%: 油菜收割,金色褪去
- 50%: 插秧季节,水田如镜,倒映天光(中点农忙)
- 54%: 梅雨季节,青石板路湿润,撑油纸伞的身影
- 57%: 荷花初绽,夏意渐浓
━━━ 夏季:小暑大暑 (57-70%)
六月酷暑,南湖荷花盛开,清香远溢,
村民在古树下乘凉,摇蒲扇,孩童湖中戏水,
祠堂举办荷花诗会,文人雅集,
晒秋场晾晒辣椒、笋干,色彩斑斓,
傍晚纳凉,露天放映老电影,
浓绿荷粉色调,生活气息浓郁,夏日慢时光。
━━━ 夏秋过渡:处暑白露 (70-82%)
- 70%: 荷花凋谢,莲蓬采摘
- 75%: 晒秋更盛,屋顶晾晒作物如调色板
- 80%: 香樟树叶渐黄,秋风起
- 82%: 准备中秋,村口搭台唱戏
━━━ 秋季:寒露霜降 (82-100%)
九月晒秋盛景,屋顶晾晒玉米、柿子、辣椒,
如上帝打翻的调色板,航拍必拍,
香樟树金黄,落叶铺满石板路,
南湖秋水澄澈如镜,倒映火红枫叶,
祠堂举办丰收节,村民感恩祖先庇佑,
中秋夜,月沼边赏月,一家团圆,
金黄橙红色调,丰收喜悦,岁月静好,圆满祥和。
━━━ 技术要求 ━━━
- 徽派建筑精准:马头墙、天井、木雕细节考究
- 农事节气对应:每个季节的农活符合真实物候
- 民俗仪式还原:祭祖、舞龙、诗会的场面真实
- 人物服饰考究:从棉袄→单衣→短袖→长衫的自然过渡
- 水体镜像强化:南湖倒影是徽州特色,需精细渲染
- 晒秋色彩爆炸:秋季屋顶农作物色彩是视觉高潮
8K,32:9,纪实摄影+中国山水画美学,《舌尖上的中国》影像质感
```

```
** Fill the specific template below.
# Constraint Checklist & Confidence Score:
1. Is the format continuous horizontal (16:9)? Yes.
2. Is the composition an S-Curve? Yes.
3. Is the Author shown as a Red Seal (Stamp)? Yes.
4. Is the Poem Title fixed at Top Right? Yes.
5. Does each scene correspond to one line of the poem? Yes.
# Output Format (Strictly follow this structure):
**[Role]** Expert Children's Book Illustrator. Style: Mixed Media (Watercolor wash & Colored Pencil details). Atmosphere: Healing, Whimsical, Fairy Tale.
**[Task]** A continuous horizontal illustration of the Tang Poem "{Poem Title}" by {Author Name}.
**[Layout & Typography]**
* **Composition:** A flowing S-curve timeline dividing the image into 4 sequential scenes blended seamlessly.
* **Title Placement:** Top Right corner, elegant layout.
* **Author Signature:** **IMPORTANT:** A distinct **Traditional Chinese Red Seal (Square Vermilion Chop/Stamp)** containing the character for "{Author Name}" placed clearly next to the title.
**[Visual Narrative Structure]**
**Scene 1 (Top Left - Start):** * **Poem Line:** "{Insert Chinese Line 1}"
* **Visual Interpretation:** {Describe the specific imagery for this line. Key objects, weather, lighting. Create a natural "Blank Space" (like clear sky/mist) for text placement.}
**Scene 2 (Middle Left - Flowing Down):** * **Poem Line:** "{Insert Chinese Line 2}"
* **Visual Interpretation:** {Describe the imagery. Connects to Scene 1. Create a "Container" (like a scroll, ripples, or wall) for text placement.}
**Scene 3 (Middle Right - The Turn/Climax):** * **Poem Line:** "{Insert Chinese Line 3}"
* **Visual Interpretation:** {Describe the climax/action. Swirling textures. Reserve a soft glowing area for text.}
**Scene 4 (Bottom Right - Resolution):** * **Poem Line:** "{Insert Chinese Line 4}"
* **Visual Interpretation:** {Describe the ending/fading view. Peaceful. Text should appear as fading light or stardust.}
**[Art Style Details]**
* **Colors:** Dreamy Pastels, Deep Night Blues, Warm Amber bioluminescence features.
* **Texture:** Visible paper grain, soft deckled edges.
* **Character:** A tiny child character guides the viewer through the journey.
**[Parameters]** --ar 16:9 --stylize 300 --v 6.0
--------------------------------------------------
```

### 2122 - 全球比心：跨越时空的爱的桥梁

**作者**: 岚叔 ([链接](https://x.com/LufzzLiz))
**风格**: cinematic
**标签**: cinematic night lighting, hyper-realistic photographic style, 8K ultra-high-definition, connection across distance, synchronized hearts

```
哈哈，受宝玉这个启发做了些不一样的东西：
依次是：跨地域比心💗（异地恋的可以试试）、高考考场与德玛握手、爱因斯坦与三体人互动、国足助攻上帝之手😂
提示词以跨地域比心为例：
【Global Definitions】
--ar 16:9 --style raw --v 6.0 --quality 2 --no cartoon, drawing, illustration, text, logo, watermark
[Framing & Style]: 8K ultra-high-definition cinematic widescreen image, hyper-realistic photographic style, cinematic night lighting with HDR (high dynamic range). Emphasis on material textures (fabrics, glass, metal, skin translucency), environmental reflections (wet pavement reflections, screen glare), and low-noise high-ISO imaging.
[Atmosphere & Mood]: Amid the bustling energy of a sleepless metropolis lies an intimate tenderness—technology and human emotion seamlessly intertwined, surreal yet grounded in realism.
[Core Imagery]: Two individuals in distant corners of the globe are poetically connected through digital media (e.g., video call), visually and emotionally bridging physical separation to emphasize “connection across distance” and “synchronized hearts.”
[Prohibited Elements]: No cartoons, illustrations, text, watermarks, brand logos (except inherent architectural identifiers of landmarks), or excessive filters.
【Variable Definitions】
A 16:9 cinematic night scene depicting a couple separated by geography but united in spirit:
She is at [Location A | Chinese Urban Landmark], wearing [Outfit A], holding or accompanied by [Object A], situated within [Scene Detail A];
He is at [Location B | American Urban Landmark], wearing [Outfit B], holding or accompanied by [Object B], situated within [Scene Detail B].
They are engaged in [Interaction Theme]:
Her action: [Female Action Description]
His action: [Male Action Description]
At the center of the frame, their emotional bond manifests through a surreal visual synthesis:
[Action Fusion Method] (merging their gestures on a visual/energetic level)
[Emotional Conduit] (e.g., light bridge, data streams, sound waves, star trails—mediums carrying longing)
[Environmental Resonance] (iconic sounds/weather/festival elements from both cities interweaving in midair)
Nighttime backdrop characteristics:
[Location A Night Features]
[Location B Night Features]
Overall color grading:
Left side leans toward [Tone A]
Right side leans toward [Tone B]
Unified visually and emotionally through [Shared Light Source / Emotional Anchor].
【Example Definition】
[Location A | Chinese Urban Landmark]: The Bund riverside promenade, Shanghai
[Outfit A]: Cream-white turtleneck sweater + camel overcoat
[Object A]: Hot milk tea, closed travel journal, AirPods
[Scene Detail A]: Leaning against cast-iron railings, hair gently tousled by river breeze
[Location B | American Urban Landmark]: Times Square crossroads, New York City
[Outfit B]: Dark gray hoodie + jeans
[Object B]: Iced Americano in paper cup, backpack adorned with NYC badge
[Scene Detail B]: Leaning against a red fire hydrant, neon lights casting glow on his profile
[Interaction Theme]: Sending love across distance via heart-shaped hand gesture
[Female Action Description]: Right hand holding phone close to cheek, gaze focused; left hand raised toward camera, palm facing outward, thumb and index finger delicately forming half a heart
[Male Action Description]: Left hand holding coffee cup at his side; right hand lifting phone high; simultaneously extending left arm with fingers spread wide, confidently forming the other half of a heart
[Action Fusion Method]: Their heart-shaped gestures perfectly overlap along the central axis of the frame, fingertips touching and emitting a soft golden glow
[Emotional Conduit]: The heart shape is composed of layered digital elements—a WeChat message bubble saying “Are you there?”, the album cover of their shared playlist City of Stars, and an unsent text bubble reading “I miss you”
[Environmental Resonance]: The Yangtze River ferry’s horn and a New York saxophone melody intertwine into circular sound waves that gently orbit the glowing heart
[Location A Night Features]: LED facades of Lujiazui skyscrapers shifting in blue, violet, and gold; laser beams from the Oriental Pearl Tower sweeping the night sky
[Location B Night Features]: Massive scrolling LED billboards in Times Square; Coca-Cola’s signature red light dancing across rain-slicked pavement
[Tone A]: Azure + Amber
[Tone B]: Magenta + Electric Blue
[Shared Light Source / Emotional Anchor]: A single full moon hangs high above both cities, its cool silver light bathing both figures’ shoulders and hair
Cinematic Split-Heart Concept – High-End Film Treatment
[Core Concept]
Using cinematic split-screen technique, two lovers separated by continents are framed within the most iconic nocturnal landscapes of their respective cities. An invisible seam runs down the center of the image. Each extends one hand toward this seam, precisely joining to form a complete heart. The essence lies in “unity within contrast”—different ambient temperatures, different levels of urban chaos—yet sharing one emotion and one moon.
[Left Side: Shanghai – Serenity Amidst Radiance]
[Location A | Establishing Shot]: Riverside platform along the Bund, flanked by the historic colonial architecture.
[Ambience A]: Rain-freshened pavement mirrors the “cyberpunk” glow of Pudong’s skyline across the Huangpu River—dominated by blue-violet hues with golden accents. A thin mist hovers over the river, softening the sharp silhouettes of skyscrapers and adding atmospheric depth.
Key Details: Water droplets cling to antique cast-iron railings, shimmering with reflected city lights.
[Character A – Female Protagonist]
Elevated Styling: Oversized cashmere coat in warm grey, layered over a finely textured silk-blend turtleneck. Minimalist metallic thread-thin earrings.
Narrative Props: Not ordinary milk tea—but a designer insulated travel tumbler; a well-worn leather-bound travel journal lies half-open beside a vintage film camera.
Key Gesture (Left Half of Heart): Standing in three-quarter profile, gazing tenderly toward the lens (as if looking directly at him). Her left arm rises gracefully, fingers naturally curved to form the left half of a heart. Her fingertips lightly touch the central dividing line.
[Right Side: New York – Chaos Infused with Warmth]
[Location B | Establishing Shot]: Rain-dampened streets of Times Square near the Broadway intersection.
[Ambience B]: Overwhelming sensory intensity. Giant LED billboards cast elongated, prismatic reflections on flooded asphalt—dominated by Coca-Cola red and Broadway gold. Background blurred: streaks of yellow cabs and silhouettes of pedestrians under umbrellas.
Key Details: Steam rising from a subway grate is dyed iridescent by surrounding neon, creating dynamic, colorful vapor trails.
[Character B – Male Protagonist]
Streetwear Aesthetic: Heavyweight washed denim jacket over a charcoal-gray hoodie (hood partially up), paired with structured work pants and scuffed leather boots.
Narrative Props: A well-used camera bag slung over one shoulder; a nearly empty coffee cup in hand (with handwritten notes on the sleeve); noise-canceling headphones draped around his neck.
Key Gesture (Right Half of Heart): Leaning against a lamppost or signal box, body slightly forward, eyes locked with quiet intensity. His right arm extends outward, mirroring her pose exactly to complete the right half of the heart. His fingertips meet hers precisely at the central axis.
[Visual Fusion Point | The Connection]
Perfect Alignment: A subtle vertical seam divides the frame. At this precise boundary, her left hand and his right hand join flawlessly—forming one complete, symmetrical heart.
Energetic Interaction (Replacing Physical Contact):
Instead of a flash of light, the point of contact generates a subtle optical distortion and faint warm-toned electric current. It feels as though the magnetic fields of two cities converge here—causing the very air at the seam to vibrate softly. A delicate golden rim light outlines the completed heart, emanating warmth without overpowering the realism.
Unified Emotional Anchor:
Though ground-level lighting diverges dramatically (left: cool blue-violet; right: vibrant red-gold), above them both, clouds part to reveal a single, luminous full moon. Its serene, silvery radiance falls equally on their shoulders and hair—and gently illuminates the heart they’ve built together across space and time.
```

### 2123 - 女儿与长颈鹿梦幻乐园

**作者**: 岚叔 ([链接](https://x.com/LufzzLiz))
**风格**: 3d
**标签**: children's illustration, miniature toy landscape, giraffe, panoramic banner, 3D title

```
女儿之前通过认知卡片认识了长颈鹿，今天上班前追着问我要看长颈鹿，答应回家给她看，大家看OK否😊
技巧分享：提示词（分两部分：全局+可配置区）：
只需要自定义可配置区，或者让AI协助你生成不同风格的可配置区（这次把主题、场景、风格、色彩、物件、氛围基调等，都抽到可配置区了，可玩度非常高，欢迎评论区交作业）
具体提示词如下：
**[Global Generation Prompt]**
A visually vivid and content-rich children's illustration themed “[SCENE_THEME],” rendered in a wide panoramic banner format (21:9 aspect ratio), depicting an exceptionally detailed miniature toy landscape. The entire scene is executed in [ART_STYLE], resembling an enormous, meticulously arranged physical miniature toy world. Lighting is bright, warm, and pure, enhanced with subtle volumetric lighting effects to create an unparalleled sense of comfort, healing, and tactile tangibility.
**I. Scene Atmosphere**
- **Target Audience:** [TARGET_AGE]
- **Current State:** [STATE_HINT]
- **Color Palette:** [COLOR_PALETTE]
- **Main Title Design:** Across the top of the image, a horizontal 3D title reads: “[SCENE_THEME] Great Discovery.” The typography must fully integrate with the [ART_STYLE] materiality (e.g., sculpted clay, plush fabric stitching, or layered paper craft). Each letter should occupy approximately 1/12 of the image height, feature extremely rounded edges, and be colored using high-saturation accent hues from the [COLOR_PALETTE]. Letter spacing should be generous, and small decorative elements (e.g., balloons, stars, musical notes) may be embedded within or around the letters. Absolutely no flat printed fonts or digital typefaces are permitted.
**II. Composition & Perspective**
- **Viewpoint:** Wide-angle miniature landscape (panoramic wide-banner format)
- **Depth of Field:** Foreground and midground rendered in sharp, crisp focus; only the farthest background slightly softened with gentle bokeh
- **Layout Principle:** Objects grouped in staggered arrangements with comfortable “breathing space”—dense yet orderly, never chaotic
**III. Core Content Elements**
- **Large Play Structures (12 items):** [CORE_OBJECTS]
All structures designed as anthropomorphized giraffes—necks serving as support pillars, spots as decorative patterns, and ears transformed into rooftops or flags.
- **Labeling Requirement:** Each structure must feature a miniature 3D label affixed to its top or side, displaying 2–3 keyword characters naming the attraction (e.g., “Ferris Wheel,” “Carousel”). Label materials must match the [ART_STYLE] (e.g., molded clay, wooden tags, or fabric sewn badges). Labels must not exceed 1/10 the size of the main structure, have fully rounded edges, use a handcrafted bold-rounded typeface, and employ high-saturation accent colors from the [COLOR_PALETTE]. Labels must feel like organic, inseparable parts of the structures—no flat printing or digital fonts allowed.
- **Small Decorative Elements (16 items):** [DETAIL_OBJECTS]
- **Environmental Base Elements (5 items):** [ENV_ELEMENTS]
**IV. Guide Characters (Non-Focal)**
Two soft, rounded, and endearing cartoon guides naturally integrated into the scene:
- A human baby wearing a pink hat
- A sky-pink bunny
Both are holding hands and gesturing animatedly toward a distant giraffe-themed Ferris wheel. Their poses serve purely as visual guidance—never drawing primary attention or overshadowing the main setting.
**V. Style & Rendering Specifications**
- **Material Consistency:** Entire scene strictly adheres to the physical properties of [ART_STYLE]
- **Edge Treatment:** All forms highly rounded; absolutely no sharp angles (minimum curvature radius > 0.3)
- **Lighting System:** Soft global illumination combined with localized volumetric lighting; no hard shadows or stark contrast
- **Render Quality:** 8K ultra-high definition, modeled in Cinema 4D style, rendered with Octane Renderer with ray tracing enabled
- **Surface Texture:** May include subtle film grain (simulating physical toy photography), but overall appearance remains clean and fresh
**VI. Negative Exclusions (Strictly Prohibited)**
- **Exception Clause:** Only the main title “[SCENE_THEME] Great Discovery” at the top and the miniature labels on large play structures are permitted.
- **Absolutely Forbidden Elements:**
No text, no labels, no numbers, no Chinese characters, no English words, no signage, no sharp edges, no realistic textures, no dark shadows, no photographic elements, no watermarks, no gradient fills on primary objects, no cluttered composition, no fused or broken forms, no detailed human faces, no digital UI elements, and no modern logos.
[Configurable Variables Section]
[SCENE_THEME] = Giraffe Cloud Sweet Dreamland
[TARGET_AGE] = 2–5 years old (vocabulary explosion stage)
[ART_STYLE] = Soft, rounded clay-sculpted 3D aesthetic (Soft Claymation / Plush 3D Sculpture)—surfaces with a subtly matte, velvety texture; edges fluffy like marshmallows; lighting and shadows gentle with no sharp angles
[COLOR_PALETTE] = Dominated by dreamy pastel pinks and mists—cherry blossom pink, creamy cloud white, soft lavender, and peachy glow—with pearlescent sheen and delicate gold accents (used on star pendants, bows, magic wand tips, and other fine details)
[VARIATION_ID] = D01
[CORE_OBJECTS] =Giraffe Marshmallow Slide – A spiral slide shaped like coiled pink gummy candy
Giraffe Bubble Tea Cups – Translucent jelly-textured cups that release fragrant, scented bubbles as they spin
Giraffe Cloud Bounce Island – A trampoline floating 30 cm above ground on plush, billowy clouds
Giraffe Starlight Swing – Swings that scatter ephemeral glittering stardust when in motion
Giraffe Dream Train – Train cars made of giant macarons and strawberries; wheels are soft donuts
Giraffe Candy Fountain Pool – Sprays edible-grade sweet mist with a hint of strawberry fragrance
Giraffe Plush Gift Tower – Automatically dispenses smiling plush ball toys
Giraffe Storybook Cottage – Flipping pages project 3D story characters into the air
Giraffe Petal Drift River – Gentle ride on lotus-petal boats drifting slowly downstream
Giraffe Goodnight Rocking Chair Zone – Rocking chairs that play soft lullabies; overhead, rotating moon lamps cast a calming glow
[DETAIL_OBJECTS] =Glowing star pendants (emit a soft “ding~” chime when touched)
Fluffy bunny-ear headbands (provide gentle warmth upon wearing, mimicking a soft hug)
Rainbow sprinkle floor decals (play crisp musical notes when stepped on)
Dreamy unicorn balloons (helium-filled, trailing shimmering ribbons)
Gummy butterflies (perch on shoulders and flutter wings gently)
Heart-shaped cloud cushions (memory foam filled, printed with smiling giraffe faces)
Moonlight seashell wind chimes (play music-box melodies in the breeze)
Magic wish bottles (glow softly when spoken to)
Strawberry milkshake mist sprayers (periodically release safe, sweet-scented fog)
Star sticker walls (repositionable stickers that leave no residue when removed)
[ENV_ELEMENTS] =Fluffy Cloud Carpet Floor – High-density soft gel material simulating the sensation of walking on clouds
Gradient Pink-to-Purple Sky Canopy – Transitioning from dawn cherry blossom pink to twilight lavender
Floating Petals & Light Particles – Slowly rising, dreamlike motes resembling particles from a sweet slumber
Ambient Glow Light Strip – Encircling soft lighting mimicking sunset afterglow, with zero glare
Invisible Safety Barrier – Wrapped in pink-and-white striped plush fabric, resembling oversized lollipop wrappers
Background Soundscape System – A soothing blend of gentle piano notes, birdsong, distant wind chimes, and soft, affectionate giraffe murmurs
[STATE_HINT] =
Afternoon sunlight filters through the pink-purple sky canopy, bathing the entire playground in the warm, milky glow of strawberry milk. Giraffes adorned with bows wave invitingly from the top of the marshmallow slide. Barefoot children scamper across the cloud carpet, chasing luminous bubbles—their laughter transforming into strings of rose-gold musical notes that float upward into the dreamy sky. Here, time has no meaning—only softness, sweetness, and endless, blissful dreams that never wake.
```

### 2126 - 宝宝识图系列：女儿专属小兔子认知大图

**作者**: 岚叔 ([链接](https://x.com/LufzzLiz))
**风格**: 3d
**标签**: 黏土捏塑, 儿童认知, 微缩景观, 双语标签, 果园大世界

```
【宝宝识图系列】女儿周末又来催更认知大图了，供不应求了，趁周末再给她做了几个系列，这次把她的形象融入了进去，宠物换成了她喜欢的小兔子，提示词尝试宝玉@dotey推荐的思路：与AI共同创作提示词：模版+实例的形式。 分享四季果园图，体现当季水果，创作过程发现Gemini不太稳定，尤其是标签总会有标错的情况，推友们有没有好的办法，可评论区一起讨论下（目前找到了一种相对稳定的办法，晚点在评论区分享）。
提示词如下：
元提示词（模版）：
[SCENE_THEME] = 果园大世界 #{季节/主题变体}
[TARGET_AGE] = 2-5岁（词汇爆发期）
[VARIATION_ID] = {编号}
[CORE_OBJECTS] = {12个纯净名词大型物体}
[DETAIL_OBJECTS] = {16个纯净名词小型物体}
[ENV_ELEMENTS] = {3-5个纯净名词环境元素}
[STATE_HINT] = {场景状态描述，仅氛围参考，不写入标签}
字体不要有错乱、乱码
【全局设置】
一幅视觉鲜明、内容丰富的儿童认知插图，主题为"[SCENE_THEME]"，宽幅全景横幅（21:9画幅），呈现一个细节极其丰富的玩具微缩景观。整体风格为柔和圆润的黏土捏塑3D美学（Soft Claymation / 绒感3D雕塑），如同一个巨大而精心布置的实体玩具微缩景观。光线明亮、温暖、纯净，带有细腻的体积光效果，营造出无与伦比的治愈感和可触摸感。
I. 报纸标题区（顶部横幅）
主标题：横向展开于画面顶部——"[SCENE_THEME] 双语认知大发现"。
字体风格：超大号、饱满多彩的黏土气球字体，边缘柔和并带有高光，仿佛漂浮在空中的棉花糖。每个字母独立成型，厚度超过3厘米。
装饰元素：标题两侧散落着与[SCENE_THEME]相关的{3个特定装饰物}小巧可爱黏土浮雕装饰。
II. 报纸主体部分（核心场景与构图）
场景氛围：一个广阔、鲜活且细节丰富的"玩具微缩世界"。色彩保持柔和的莫兰迪/马卡龙色调，同时具备高饱和度。当前场景状态：[STATE_HINT]
构图要求：
- 采用宽幅全景横幅广角微缩景观（Wide-angle Miniature Landscape）视角。
- 景深调整：前景与中景完全清晰锐利；仅最远处背景做轻微柔焦处理。
- 布局策略：所有物体分组并分散布置于画面不同位置，各物体之间保留舒适的"呼吸空间"，避免视觉杂乱。
引导角色：
2位圆润可爱的黏土卡通向导——参考图片人物样貌（戴粉色小帽）与可爱的小兔子（天粉色身体），通过夸张而清晰的肢体语言（如手指指向、抬头仰望）引导视线扫视整幅画面。
III. 必备物体与认知清单（本系列第[VARIATION_ID]期）
**核心认知大型物体（12个）：**
[CORE_OBJECTS]
**丰富认知小型物体（16个）：**
[DETAIL_OBJECTS]
**环境元素（背景与地面装饰）：**
[ENV_ELEMENTS]
IV. 精准双语标签系统（核心防乱码优化）
- **样式**：圆形厚实黏土质感的三行标签牌，直径约8-10厘米，底色柔和米白（RGB 250,245,240），轻微3D浮雕效果，如磁贴吸附在画面上。标签牌边缘有2毫米厚的彩色描边（颜色与物体主色调呼应）。
- **字体要求**：
- 第一行拼音：使用"站酷快乐体"风格，字号24pt，颜色深灰（RGB 60,60,60），字符间距0
- 第二行中文：使用"方正粗圆简体"风格，字号36pt加粗，颜色纯黑（RGB 30,30,30），字符间距0
- 第三行英文：使用"Comic Sans MS"圆润字体，字号20pt，颜色深灰（RGB 70,70,70），字符间距0
- **渲染指令**：文字必须作为矢量图形直接嵌入渲染，禁用位图转换。每个字符单独建模为黏土浮雕字母，确保边缘锐利无锯齿，抗锯齿等级最高，渲染采样率≥2000。
- **位置规则**：每个标签位于对应物体正下方5-8厘米处，标签之间不重叠，整体呈网格状分布。标签牌用半透明细线（不显示在最终画面）与物体连接。
V. 风格参数与渲染指令
- **风格基调**：黏土动画微缩景观
- **关键修饰词**：拥挤但有序的构图、精准标注、柔和体积光、超清晰文字渲染
- **色彩与材质**：丰富柔和的粉彩色系、可触摸的黏土质感、光滑圆润边缘（曲率半径>0.3）
- **渲染质量**：8K超高清，Cinema 4D可爱渲染风格，Octane渲染器，光线追踪
- **文字渲染**：启用矢量文字直接嵌入，禁止位图化文字，抗锯齿等级最高
VI. 负面提示（严格排除）
no sharp edges, no realistic textures, no dark shadows, no small text, no cluttered composition, **no quantity numbers displayed on objects**, **no Chinese character corruption**, **no pixelated text**, **no blurry fonts**, no watermarks, no photographic elements, no gradient text, no broken letters, no fused characters, no incomplete glyphs
示例提示词：（其他季节及当季水果可以让AI协助生成）：
[SCENE_THEME] = 春季开花果园 [VARIATION_ID] = 01 [STATE_HINT] = 花朵盛开，整体色调粉白为主，树木以花为主 [CORE_OBJECTS] = 杏树、李子树、草莓田、蓝莓丛、樱桃树、柑橘树、枇杷树、蜜蜂箱、红色拖拉机、蓝色洒水车、白色储藏小屋、绿色稻草人 [DETAIL_OBJECTS] = 蜜蜂、草莓、杏子、白蝴蝶、啄木鸟、园丁小桶、浇水壶、圆润石头、蚯蚓、瓢虫、蜗牛、飘舞花瓣、园艺手套、小草帽、雨靴、喷壶 [ENV_ELEMENTS] = 棉花糖云朵、弹跳小河、圆润山丘、彩虹弧线、彩色石子小路 标题装饰：小花、蜜蜂、水滴
```

### 2127 - 2-5岁宝宝认知大发现：3D黏土风全景长图

**作者**: 岚叔 ([链接](https://x.com/LufzzLiz))
**风格**: 3d
**标签**: 黏土塑形, 微缩景观, 双语认知, 视觉冲击力, 全景长图

```
Structure)
【全局设定】
一张极具视觉冲击力、内容丰富的儿童认知横幅全景长图 (Wide Panoramic Banner)，主题为《[SCENE_THEME]》，竖版 A4。整体采用柔和、圆润的黏土塑形 3D 风格 (Soft Claymation/Plush 3D Sculpture)，就像一个巨大的、精心布置的实体玩具沙盘。光线明亮、温暖、纯净，带有细腻的体积光，营造出无与伦比的治愈感和触摸感。
一、小报标题区（顶部横幅）
大标题：在画面顶部横向展开标题：《[SCENE_THEME] 双语认知大发现》。
字体风格：超大、圆滚滚的彩色黏土气球字体，边缘带有柔和的描边和高光，像浮在空中的棉花糖。
装饰元素：标题两侧散落着与 [SCENE_THEME] 相关的可爱小型黏土装饰浮雕，增加横幅的热闹感。
二、小报主体（核心场景与构图）
场景气氛：一个广阔、充满活力且细节丰富的“玩具微缩世界”。色彩依然保持莫兰迪/马卡龙色系的柔和高饱和度，但画面信息量大幅增加。
构图要求（关键变化）：
采用广角微缩景观 (Wide-angle Miniature Landscape) 视角。
景深调整：不再是极浅景深。前景和中景（放置大量物体的主要区域）必须全焦段清晰锐利。只有最远处的背景才进行柔焦虚化处理，以交代环境氛围。
布局策略：由于物件增多，需要将物体分组、分区域散落在横幅的不同位置，物体之间保持舒适的“呼吸感”间距，避免视觉拥挤。
引导角色：
1-2 位圆润可爱的黏土卡通向导（如：探险家宝宝和机器小狗），在场景中处于活跃状态，用夸张、清晰的肢体语言引导视线浏览整个横幅。
三、必画物体与认知清单 (Rich Content Generation)
请在广阔的横幅场景中，清晰、散点式地分布以下各类物体。所有物体都必须是极度圆润、无尖角、黏土玩具质感：
核心认知大件 (Major Objects)：
（数量增加到 5-8 个 大型标志性物体，分布在场景主要位置）
{在此填入 5-8 个核心大物体，例如：霸王龙、三角龙、火山、翼龙巢穴、原始人部落}
丰富认知小件 (Detailed Items)：
（数量增加到 8-12 个 中小型具体物品，散落在核心大件周围）
{在此填入 8-12 个具体小物品，例如：恐龙蛋、巨大的骨头、蕨类植物、小蜥蜴、石斧、蜻蜓}
环境元素 (Environmental Elements)：
（用于丰富背景和地面的元素，数量不限，但要简化）
{例如：圆润的石块、Q弹的河流、棉花糖般的云朵}
四、精准指示与双语标签系统 (Precise Labeling System)这是本版优化的核心，用于处理多物体认知带来的混乱感。
对上述所有需要认知的物体进行标注，必须严格遵守以下规则：
标签样式 (Label Box Style)：
一个圆角矩形、具有厚实黏土或软胶质感的三行标签牌。底色为柔和的奶白色或浅黄色，以突出文字。
标签牌带有微微的 3D 浮雕效果，像贴在画面上的精美磁铁。
标签内容 (Three-Line Content)：
第一行：简体汉字（超粗圆体，颜色醒目）。
第二行：标准汉语拼音（带声调，字体稍小，清晰易读）。
第三行：英文单词（清晰的无衬线圆体，字体稍小）。
示例格式：
[ 恐 龙 ]
[ kǒng lóng ]
[ Dinosaur ]
精准指示箭头 (Precise Pointing Arrow)：
形态：必须使用一根粗壮、圆润、像挤出的牙膏或黏土条一样的 3D 箭头。颜色采用醒目的暖色调（如橙黄色或粉蓝色），与背景形成对比。
连接方式：箭头的一端连接标签牌边缘，另一端非常精准地、就近地指向或触碰对应的目标物体。
布局原则：箭头必须清晰、顺畅，严禁箭头之间相互交叉。标签牌应放置在离物体最近的空白区域，通过箭头连接，确保画面整洁有序。
五、画风参数总结 (Style Parameters Summary)
风格基调：Wide Panoramic Claymation Diorama for Toddlers (幼儿黏土透视场景)
关键修饰：Crowded but Organized Composition (丰富但有序的构图), Precise Labeling with Thick 3D Arrows (带粗 3D 箭头的精准标注), Soft Volumetric Lighting (柔和体积光).
色彩与质感：Rich Pastel Colors, Tactile Clay Texture, Smooth Edges.
渲染质量：8K Ultra HD, Cinema 4D cute render styles.
```

```
连接方式：箭头的一端连接标签牌边缘，另一端非常精准地、就近地指向或触碰对应的目标物体。
布局原则：箭头必须清晰、顺畅，严禁箭头之间相互交叉。标签牌应放置在离物体最近的空白区域，通过箭头连接，确保画面整洁有序。
五、画风参数总结 (Style Parameters Summary)
风格基调：Vertical A4 Claymation Diorama for Toddlers (竖版 A4 幼儿黏土透视场景)
关键修饰：Crowded but Organized Composition (丰富但有序的构图), Precise Labeling with Thick 3D Arrows (带粗 3D 箭头的精准标注), Soft Volumetric Lighting (柔和体积光).
色彩与质感：Rich Pastel Colors, Tactile Clay Texture, Smooth Edges.
渲染质量：8K Ultra HD, Cinema 4D cute render styles.
```

**风格**: 3d

```
《3D微缩国风叙事》
最近有个杂志社客户联系我，想要给小学生做一些历史人物、名篇的卡牌、插画。
偶然刷到@ykszs017 这位推友的二创，觉得很合适古诗词，于是结合@dotey 宝玉老师的原始提示词，做了一些融合改造，现在只需要输入诗篇名字，就能直出这种3D微缩国风叙事风格了。

提示词：
Present a high-fidelity, 45° top-down isometric miniature diorama, crafted with premium PBR materials, delicate sculpted details, and warm cinematic lighting.
The entire scene should feel like a finely carved, hand-crafted poetic model—rich in texture, soft in atmosphere, and emotionally immersive.
Focus the composition on the core imagery and symbolic environment extracted from the input poem—such as roads, rivers, bridges, cottages, mountains, moonlight, seasons, travelers, solitude, or nostalgia.
All elements should appear stylized yet realistic, with slightly exaggerated miniature proportions to enhance storytelling.
Use layered depth, subtle fog, volumetric light beams, and warm sunset or soft daylight tones to create strong emotional resonance.
Textures must be refined and tactile: aged wood, weathered stone, soft grass, dried reeds, drifting leaves, flowing water with gentle refraction, and carved signage.
Maintain a clean, unified vertical 9:16 composition with a soft solid background that isolates the diorama and highlights the scene.
文字呈现方式（必备）：
- At the top-center, display the poem title in large, elegant text.
- Beneath it, show the poet name in x-small text.
- Within the diorama, organically integrate one to two famous lines from the poem, using calligraphic vertical text placed on stone tablets, tree bark, hanging banners, wooden planks, drifting mist, or carved signs—
ensuring they blend naturally and enhance narrative depth.
All text must match the poem’s original language and appear physically present within the world.
渲染风格要求：
- warm cinematic lighting
- sculptural fidelity
- ultra-refined micro-details
- soft ambient occlusion
- subtle rim light on characters and objects
- strong storytelling through posing and shadow direction
- cohesive color palette, desaturated yet warm
Before rendering, analyze the poem to extract:
- iconic setting
- emotional tone
- symbolic elements
- season & time of day
- narrative tension or longing
Transform the poem into a miniature 3D poetic world with deep atmosphere and emotional weight.
灵感来源：
https://t.co/MHppcxgQB8
原始提示词：
https://t.co/AMHAovJer1
```

```
On the right side ({Era_B}): show the same {Scene} in the modern era, with updated architecture or renovated structures, contemporary materials (glass, steel, LED screens, modern furniture), modern vehicles or equipment, and current technology (smartphones, laptops, cameras, etc.). People wear contemporary fashion that matches today’s style in this setting.
In the center: the two eras merge and overlap organically, without a hard dividing line. Elements from {Era_A} and {Era_B} visually interact: people from different times look at each other, walk through each other’s space, or seem surprised by the other era’s technology and objects. Architecture and environment smoothly morph from old to new (for example, stone gates turning into modern campus gates, classical concert hall décor fading into a futuristic stage, old street shops transforming into neon-lit storefronts).
Make sure the scene is not just a simple left/right comparison but a dynamic time-travel interaction where buildings, clothing, props, and human gestures clearly emphasize the contrast and fusion between the two eras. В західних соцмережах набирає обертів новий тренд, який пов'язаний з релізом оновленої версії нейромережі  Nano Banana Pro. 🍌Nano Banana  Pro 的上线依然还在提高！
```
```
提示词：为你的家乡做张城市海报
所有的元素都会自动根据你选择城市进行调整：
比如云海的艺术风格、3D 字体上面的装饰、经纬度、建成时间、别称、独特的鸟类和生物、天际线景观和古建筑
提示词：
一张针对 [城市名称] 的城市渲染数字艺术海报。
画面核心主体是一个漂浮在白云上方、形状像所选城市的并且占据画面大部分内容的微型岛屿。岛屿的形状与城市在地图上的形状相似，无缝融合城市独特的标志性地标、自然景观及文化元素。加入城市特有的鸟类、电影般的光影、鲜艳色彩、航拍视角和阳光反射效果，建筑不宜太多太密集。
岛屿展现历史与现代的无缝融合。一部分是该城市最具代表性的古代历史建筑；另一部分平滑过渡为城市的地标建筑和天际线景观。
```

```
第一步，智能识别与场景构建： 请先调用你的知识库，判断我输入的主题属于哪种类别（是虚构文学、真实历史事件、体育竞技还是科学发现）。 然后，精准检索该主题发生时的具体环境、核心人物动作、服饰细节以及当时的气候氛围。 在画面中央，建立一个精致的3D微缩场景模型。风格上，保持梦工厂动画那种高精度的材质感和柔和的光影渲染。 如果是体育，要强调动作的爆发力和凝固的瞬间感；如果是历史，要还原年代感和严肃性；如果是文学，则强调意境。

第三步，排版与文字： 在底部居中位置生成中文排版。 主标题写上事件或作品的名称，字体设计要契合主题风格。 在标题下方，请自动检索该事件最著名的金句、解说词、日期或历史评价，字体使用优雅的衬线体。整体布局要像一个高级的博物馆藏品铭牌那样精致平衡。
```

### 2541 - 纳米香蕉进阶之路海报

**作者**: 歸藏(guizang.ai) ([链接](https://x.com/op7418))
**风格**: 3d
**标签**: 黏土风格, 前端工程师, 游戏关卡地图, 进阶之路, 任天堂风格

```
🍌 Nano Banana Pro 提示词：任何爱好或者职业的进阶之路
用非常可爱的黏土风格展示不同等级的标准和消耗时间
-------提示词--------
基于主题 [前端工程师的进阶之路]，创作一张 3D 游戏关卡地图海报。
画面结构： 一条蜿蜒曲折的 3D 道路从画面底部延伸至顶部云端，分为三个主要的“关卡阶段”：
底部：新手村 (Level 1: Noob)
模型： 简单的草地场景。放置基础工具。
路标： 插着木牌，写着标题，下方用一段话介绍当前等级的标准。
中部：试炼场 (Level 10: Pro)
模型： 地形变得复杂（森林或岩石）。放置进阶装备。
视觉： 道路变得陡峭，象征难度增加。
路标： 插着木牌，写着标题，下方用一段话介绍当前等级的标准。
顶部：神之殿 (Level 99: Master)
模型： 漂浮在云端的辉煌神殿或高科技实验室。放置终极神器。
视觉： 发着金光，有彩虹或宝箱。
```

```
Minyatür tarzı (User request doldurulacak)
You are an expert in Ottoman miniature painting (Nakkaşhane) style. The user will enter only a single-line request. Without asking any follow-up questions, you will complete all missing creative and technical decisions internally and transform that request into a single visual scene in authentic Ottoman miniature aesthetics.
Input (user request):
{USER_REQUEST}
Internal process (do not show to the user, just apply):
1.Analyze the request and automatically determine: the main event, main characters/figures, setting/location, time period, and cultural context. If the user does not specify a time, choose the most iconic and recognizable period that best fits the subject.
2.Period accuracy is mandatory:
•Clothing, hair, accessories, stage/environment objects, vehicles, signage, architecture, and crowd behavior must be consistent with the chosen era.
•For concert/event scenes: stage layout, instruments, lighting system, speaker stacks, poster aesthetic (without text), crowd clothing style, and safety barriers must match the era.
•Example rule: If the user says “Metallica,” construct a concert scene; depict era-appropriate metal concert clothing (black T-shirts, leather jackets, denim, band-merch vibe), crowd dynamics (headbanging, hands raised), and correct-looking stage gear and instruments, but rendered in miniature language (stylized, not photorealistic).
```

```
四季流转 - 动态视频镜头脚本
【冬/Winter 】
镜头运动：缓慢推进 + 轻微上摇
雪花在前景中飘落，镜头从雪覆盖的松树枝开始
逐渐推进至木屋村落，炊烟袅袅升起
溪流中残冰反射着冷蓝色的光

【冬→春过渡 】
镜头运动：快速横移扫过
画面从冷色调的冬季快速横移
雪花变成雨滴，冰雪开始消融
色温从冷蓝渐变为温暖的粉色
【春/Spring 】
镜头运动：环绕运动 + 轻微升起
镜头绕着正在消融的溪流环绕
捕捉冰雪融化成溪流的动态过程
道路从雪白变为泥土色
远处树木开始吐出新绿，薄雾弥漫
诗意文字浮现："春回大地，冰雪消融" / "Spring awakens, ice melts into streams"
【春→夏过渡 】
镜头运动：俯冲拉近
镜头快速俯冲至地面
聚焦野花从土地中绽放的过程
色彩从粉色过渡到鲜艳的绿色和蓝色
【夏/Summer 】
镜头运动：流畅的航拍移动 + 缓慢旋转
镜头以鸟瞰视角沿着溪流移动
草地上野花盛开，色彩斑斓（紫、红、黄）
溪流水波粼粼，倒映蓝天
木屋周围绿意盎然，生机勃勃
诗意文字浮现："夏日草原，牛羊成群" / "Summer pastures teem with life"
【夏→秋过渡 】
镜头运动：时间流逝特效
使用延时摄影效果
绿叶快速变黄、变红
天空从明蓝渐变为金黄
【秋/Autumn 】
镜头运动：缓慢拉远 + 向上仰拍
从木屋开始，镜头缓慢拉远
展现整片金黄的白桦林
落叶在风中飘舞
溪流倒映着金色的树林
夕阳将整个画面染成温暖的橙红色
诗意文字浮现："金秋不木，层林尽染" / "Golden autumn paints the forest"
【结尾 】
镜头运动：定格 + 光效
画面定格在四季合一的分屏构图
每个季节的色彩在边界处柔和交融
中文书法文字和英文标题淡入：
"春夏秋冬，生生不息"
"Four Seasons, Eternal Cycle"
```

```
初夏の昼・快晴の天気の中、緯度と経度が34.4059625002507, 133.1961464121396の位置で、二人の女性が観光してるように馴染ませてください。観光雑誌の表紙のように美しくレイアウトしてください。9:16をあますことなく活かし、この雑誌の表紙を作成してください。これが来客数に影響する重要なページだと思って取り組んでください。
```

### 3089 - 西游记师徒四人组成了一个摇滚乐队

**作者**: 松果先森 ([链接](https://x.com/songguoxiansen))
**风格**: artistic
**标签**: Gongbi-style, ink painting, Tang Sanzang, Sun Wukong, concert

```
A traditional Chinese Gongbi-style ink painting. The scene humorously depicts a grand concert taking place on a giant, floating lotus leaf amidst a sea of clouds. At the center, Tang Sanzang wears cool aviator sunglasses and acts as a DJ, scratching on a turntable made of ancient millstones. Beside him, Sun Wukong is in mid-air performing a heavy metal jump, shredding on a flaming electric guitar styled like a Pipa. Zhu Bajie sits on a subwoofer drum set, enthusiastically banging the drums with two rake-shaped drumsticks, sweat flying. Sha Wujing stands calmly at the back, holding a microphone stand, crooning a jazz ballad with a saxophone hanging from his neck. Traditional Chinese calligraphy lyrics float in the air, accompanied by a classic red artist’s seal inscribed "魔音穿耳".
这是一幅传统的工笔水墨画。画面生动地描绘了一场盛大的音乐会，地点设在一片巨大的漂浮荷叶之上，云海环绕。唐三藏戴着酷炫的飞行员墨镜，扮演着DJ的角色，用一块古老的磨盘打碟。在他身旁，孙悟空在空中表演着重金属跳跃，弹奏着一把形似琵琶的火焰电吉他。猪八戒坐在低音鼓上，挥舞着两根耙状鼓槌，汗流浃背地热情地敲击着鼓面。沙悟净则镇定地站在后方，手持麦克风架，脖子上挂着萨克斯，低吟着爵士情歌。传统的中国书法歌词在空中飘荡，旁边还钤着一枚经典的红色艺术家印章，上面写着“魔音穿耳”。
```

```
提示词分享：
展示一个精致的、微缩 3D 卡通风格的{场地/Location}在雪中的场景，采用清晰的 45° 俯视等轴侧视角（Isometric view）。
核心构图： 将主体最经典的形象突出地置于中心。自动搭配比例适宜的关键元素图标、象征性物品、迷人的小角色以及能诠释主体故事的道具。整体布局应当充满趣味且紧凑聚集，宛如一套高端的玩具盲盒套装。
渲染与材质： 采用Octane Render 和 Cinema 4D风格进行渲染。建模必须精细、圆润流畅且质感丰富。使用逼真的 PBR 材质：混合用于有机形态的柔和哑光粘土、用于水体/玻璃元素的光泽树脂，以及用于结构组件的光滑 PVC 材质。着重表现具有触感、“看起来手感很好”的纹理细节。
灯光与氛围： 采用柔和、逼真的摄影棚布光配合全局光照（Global Illumination）。利用柔和的阴影营造出温暖、舒适且充满魔力的氛围。
布局： 保持干净、极简的布局，使用与主体配色相协调的纯色背景。图像边缘使用与天气相关的效果，例如冬天下雪会有类似霜冻的效果
文字与图标： 在顶部中央，使用巨大的、圆润的 3D 字体醒目地展示"北京"，使其轻微悬浮于场景上方。使用极具中国风格特色的天气符号表明当前北京的温度与天气，要求使用与天气符合的字体与文字效果
图像宽高比3：4
```

### 3526 - 新海诚风格唯美背景插画对比

**作者**: VoxcatAI ([链接](https://x.com/VoxcatAI))
**风格**: anime
**标签**: 新海诚风格, 唯美背景, 积雨云, 魔幻时刻, 宽幅构图

```
随手拍的图片与生成后的图片对比
提示词👇
请为我生成一张极具「新海诚 Makoto Shinkai」动画电影风格的唯美背景插画。描绘的核心场景是：「{场景，例如：樱花盛开的铁路道口 / 雨后的新宿街头 / 黄昏时的学校天台}」，但重点不在人物，而在整个环境与天空的氛围。
整张画面采用宽幅 16:9 构图，仿佛一帧直接截自新海诚电影。天空必须是绝对主角，占据画面的大部分面积：请绘制极其壮观、细节丰富的天空，例如高耸入云的巨型积雨云塔，或橙色、紫色与深蓝渐变交织的华丽晚霞，或雨后初晴横跨城市的一道巨大彩虹。光线处于最美的魔幻时刻，可以是黄金时刻（Golden Hour）或蓝色时刻（Blue Hour），天空中有强烈却柔和的镜头光晕（lens flare），阳光从云层间隙洒落形成明显的体积光，空气里漂浮着细微的发光尘埃与光粒子。
地面与环境部分，请将「{场景}」描绘得接近照片级写实：精细刻画电线杆、复杂的电线网络、路牌、交通信号灯、自动贩卖机、远处的高楼剪影以及城市中细碎的生活痕迹。若场景包含雨或积水，地面应呈现湿润的质感，像镜子一样清晰反射出天空的色彩和周围建筑的灯光，使画面充满通透、清凉的空气感与微微的湿度。
构图上采用广角大远景，强调环境的宏大与空间纵深感，让观众有一种抬头仰望天空、被景色吞没的感觉。如果画面中出现人物，他们应该只是画面中很小的剪影或背影，站在街角、天台或铁道旁，仰望天空或远方，用微小的身影增强故事感、孤独感和对未来的想象，而不要抢走环境的主角位置。
整体色彩需清澈、饱和却不刺眼，光影对比强烈但依然柔和，细节丰富到可以直接当作桌面壁纸使用。画面风格要明显带有新海诚式的浪漫和透明感，而不是厚重油画或写实照片风格。高分辨率插画，16:9 宽屏比例。
```

### 3563 - 城市地标融合插画师：AI绘画引擎操作指南

**作者**: 神奇小喷菇AIGC ([链接](https://x.com/xpg0970))
**风格**: 3d
**标签**: 微缩城市, 地标融合, 美食场景, 3D渲染, 城市海报

```
Role: 城市地标融合画师 (City Landmark Fusion Illustrator)
1. 核心指令 (Prime Directive)
你是一个只画图、不说话的 AI 绘画引擎。
当检测到用户输入中包含以下信息时，立即启动绘图程序：
必填：日期，城市，温度，天气
选填：周几/星期信息（如：周一、星期三、Mon、Monday）
执行步骤：
静默构思：
自动检索该城市最具辨识度的地标建筑作为场景核心。
自动匹配该城市代表性本地美食 / 非遗文化 / 标志性交通工具（如：重庆火锅、轻轨，成都火锅、熊猫，北京烤鸭、胡同等）。
将「地标建筑」与「本地美食 / 文化」大胆融合在同一个奇幻微缩场景中。
直接绘图：
按下面的视觉逻辑，用 DALL·E 3 生成一张高精度 3D 微缩城市海报。
禁止废话：
不输出任何文字，只返回图片。
2. 画面视觉逻辑 (Visual Hierarchy)
A. 双主角：地标 × 美食场景 (Twin Heroes)
地位： 城市地标 + 代表性美食 共同构成画面主角。
融合表现示例：
火锅/面条/米饭可以被巨大化，变成城市地形或容器：
沸腾的火锅像红色湖泊，地标建筑建在锅沿、高台或岛屿上。
辣椒变成山体或巨型隧道，轻轨从其中穿梭而出。
地标保持清晰可辨，同时与食材、汤汁、蒸汽产生互动：
建筑被雾气包围，边缘溅有汤汁或辣椒碎。
B. 微缩人物与城市日常 (Micro Life)
在锅沿、食材表面或街道上加入微缩人物：
坐在辣椒上划船、拿公文包上班、等车、拍照等。
人物尺寸要远小于建筑与食材，营造玩具感和幽默感。
C. 城市文化与交通元素 (Culture & Transit)
根据城市特征，添加 1–2 个标志性元素：
轻轨、地铁、索道、传统牌楼、红灯笼、剪纸、皮影等。
控制数量，避免画面过于拥挤，主视线始终落在地标 + 美食上。
D. 物理环境与天气渲染 (Weather & Atmosphere)
根据输入的「天气」字段调整整体环境：
多云 / 雾 / 霾：
大量云雾或水汽从火锅/热食中翻涌，远处城市被雾气半遮掩。
雨天：
锅沿与建筑表面湿润，高光明显，地面有积水与倒影。
色调偏冷、略带蓝灰。
雪天：
建筑屋顶和锅边覆盖积雪，部分食材上也有薄薄白雪。
整体偏高调，空气通透。
晴天：
一侧有强烈阳光斜射，阴影清晰。
色调明亮、温暖。
3. 画面参数规范 (Visual Specs)
风格： 高精度 3D 渲染，微缩模型 / 手办质感；材质真实，细节丰富。
视角： 约 45° 轴测 / 俯视视角，像俯视摆在桌面上的城市模型。
构图比例： 垂直 9:16，画面下半部分为美食场景，上半部分延伸为城市天际线与天空/云雾。
4. UI 植入规则 (On-screen UI)
在画面顶部预留干净空间，以极简 UI 形式展示文字信息：
必须包含：
【天气图标】
【城市名】
【日期】
【温度】
如果用户输入了「周几/星期」信息（如：周一、星期日、Mon、Monday）：
将其一并展示在 UI 中，排版示例：
中文：重庆 · 12月2日 周一
英文：CHONGQING · Dec 2 · Monday
尽量沿用用户输入的语言形式：
输入「周一」→ 画面也显示「周一」；
输入「Monday」→ 画面显示「Monday」。
若用户没有提供周几，则不要自行推算或虚构周几，直接只显示日期。
文字风格：
UI 文本简洁、易读，不喧宾夺主。
与整体色调协调，可居中或顶部偏上布置。
5. 文本内容约束 (Text Constraints)
画面中允许出现的文字仅限：
城市名
日期
周几（如果用户输入提供）
温度
简单天气图标或天气英文单词（如 Cloudy / Sunny，可选）
不要在画面其他位置添加无关文案或标语。
6. 触发执行 (Trigger)
当接收到包含 日期、城市、温度、天气 的输入时（可附带周几），例如：
2019-12-02 周一 重庆 11°C 多云
Dec 2, 2019 · Monday · Chongqing · 11°C · Cloudy
你应当：
立即根据该城市生成对应的「地标 + 美食 + 文化/交通元素」融合场景。
按上述视觉与 UI 规则绘制海报。
不回复任何文字，只输出最终图片。
```
